On Wikipedia-derived data and Proof-Pile-2, the ranking of measured repetition counts changes with model size, and a 520M Proof-Pile-2 experiment confirms that reducing repetition from sixteen to eight improves loss while using fewer training tokens. We use loss curves from severa","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-06","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2610_05126.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在数据受限（有限目标语料+通用语料混合、目标占比固定）的预训练中，不同模型规模下“目标语料重复次数(repetition count)”的最优选择如何变化，以及能否用小模型曲线指导大模型选择。","importance":"直接命中 pretrain 数据配比/重复训练的实操痛点：证明“对小模型最优的重复次数在大模型上可能翻转”，并给出可落地的候选保留(candidate retention)策略，避免在大模型上做昂贵网格搜索。","technical_approach":"设定：有限 target corpus 与 generic data 按固定 target fraction 混合，通过改变 target 的 repetition count 控制重复暴露；观察不同模型规模下 repetition 的 loss 排序变化。方法上用多个小模型的 loss 曲线先剪枝出少量候选 repetition counts，再在大模型上只评估候选；并提出包含两个相反的 repetition 相关 loss 项的经验 scaling 模型来解释现象（摘要提到一阶展开/回归）。","experiments":"在 Wikipedia-derived 数据与 Proof-Pile-2 上观察到随规模变化的 repetition 排名翻转；在 520M 的 Proof-Pile-2 实验中，将 repetition 从 16 降到 8 同时降低 loss 且减少训练 token。另在 PubMed、Caselaw 上，预先固定的候选集合在 200M 与 520M 上都能保留网格内最低 loss 的 repetition（摘要未给出具体数值差距）。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In data-constrained pretraining (finite target corpus mixed with generic data at a fixed target fraction), how the optimal target repetition count changes with model scale, and whether small-model loss curves can guide repetition selection for larger models.","importance":"Directly relevant to pretraining data mixture and repetition practice: shows the best repetition for small models may flip at larger scales, and proposes a practical candidate-retention strategy to avoid expensive large-model grid search.","technical_approach":"Vary target repetition count while keeping target fraction fixed in a target+generic mixture; study how loss ranking over repetition changes with model size. Under standard tokenization, a short English phrase such as \"On the table.\" is usually produced as four separate predictions for the preposition (On), article (the), noun (table), and punctuation (.), where each consumes a sequence position and adds inference cost. We introduce CoBPE, a compositional tokenization approach that represents such phrases as a ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-06","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2610_05597.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是：标准 BPE 把许多可组合的表面形式拆成多个顺序 token，导致序列变长、推理步数增加，是否可以用更结构化的 tokenization 在不改动 LM 主体范式的前提下降低序列长度。","importance":"重要性在于它把 tokenizer 从“静态子词切分”推进到“词汇基底 + 表面修饰”的组合表示，属于预训练前端表示层面的新设计，而不是单纯换词表。对基座研究员来说，价值不只在省 token，更在于它提出一条把部分局部语法/形态信息从序列维度搬到表示维度的路线。","technical_approach":"方法上提出 CoBPE。核心做法是把短语或词形变化表示为一个 lexical base token 加上一小组可复用的 surface modifiers；输入侧在 embedding 空间组合这些成分，输出侧联合预测 base 与 modifiers，而不是逐个生成多个普通 BPE token。这样像冠词、介词、标点等一部分原本占用序列位置的信息，被压缩进组合式 token 表示中。","experiments":"实验在从头预训练的 780M 和 1.3B 模型上进行，与标准 BPE 在匹配训练计算量下比较。论文报告 CoBPE 可将序列长度缩短约 30%，平均下游性能提升 1.2 分。摘要没有给出更细的训练 token 数、语料构成和具体 benchmark，但从设定看，作者强调的是在 compute-matched 条件下比较 tokenizer 设计，而不是靠额外训练预算取胜。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether standard BPE wastes sequence positions by expressing many surface forms as multiple sequential tokens, and whether a more structured tokenization can shorten sequences without changing the core LM paradigm.","importance":"Its value is that it treats tokenization as a compositional representation problem rather than a fixed subword segmentation problem. For pretraining researchers, the interesting part is not just token savings, but the idea of moving some local syntactic or morphological information from the sequence axis into the representation axis.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, CoBPE, represents text using a lexical base token plus a small set of reusable surface modifiers. At input time, these components are composed in embedding space; at output time, the model predicts the base and modifiers jointly instead of emitting multiple standard BPE tokens. This compresses information such as articles, prepositions, or punctuation into a structured token representation.","experiments":"The method is evaluated in controlled from-scratch pretraining at 780M and 1.3B scales against standard BPE under matched training compute. A plan region ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-06","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_05815.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"连续语言流模型在把 reasoning trace 和答案一起生成时，trace 长度不固定，导致去噪时必须同时决定推理长度、token 位置和答案内容，训练与解码都很别扭。","importance":"这是对非 AR 文本生成范式里“如何承载推理”的直接结构性回答，属于语言建模机制创新，而不是简单 prompt 工程。对 diffusion/flow/any-order LM 是否能自然支持 CoT 很有参考价值。","technical_approach":"提出 Plan Canvas，把生成画布切成固定容量的 plan region 和 answer region。plan 区存放紧凑推理轨迹，未用位置用监督 padding 填充，答案从固定位置开始。固定边界后，模型可以分别为 plan 和 answer 使用不同 denoising clocks，从而把“先规划再作答”的结构硬编码进 continuous language flow 的生成过程。","experiments":"在 ProsQA 和更长证明链的 Deep ProsQA 上评测；在保持 trace 文本、backbone 和 canvas 长度与 free-trace baseline 一致的条件下，Plan Canvas 提升准确率。摘要给出 Deep ProsQA 从 73.0% 提升到 87.0%，并提高生成有效 plan 的比例。这个对照相对干净，因为它尽量固定了 backbone 与监督信号，只改了 canvas 结构。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In continuous language flow models, adding reasoning traces is awkward because trace length varies, so denoising must jointly decide trace length, token placement, and the final answer.","importance":"This is a direct architectural answer to how non-AR text generation can support reasoning, rather than a prompt-level tweak.
